# R10b: a realizable reservoir recursion, its all-depth obstruction, and paid face changes

**UNVERIFIED direct mathematical draft. Original minimax target ACTIVE / OPEN.**

Grant `d18f0ac50208916a`. One assigned native gpt-6-astra / ultra invocation; exactly two substantive approaches; mathematical programs, arrays, solvers, formal submissions, extra agents and extra model calls: zero. This packet does not contain a complete target-level candidate.

The main outcome is an actual backward construction using movable walls, followed by an all-depth obstruction to its entire monotone-face class. It handles an arbitrarily large finite terminal reservoir, not just the previously excluded two-point policies. The second approach pays for a real change between opposite saturated faces; it gives a recursive bank of full-word witnesses with exact radii, but its independent reserves also fail on the complete inherited hierarchy. Neither obstruction is an upper bound on the unrestricted worst-case value.

## 1. Target, sources, and notation

The required lower theorem remains

\[
 \forall\epsilon>0\ \exists N_\epsilon\ \forall n\ge N_\epsilon\quad
 \forall h\in[0,\infty)^n:\qquad
 \mathcal W_n(h)\ge(1-\epsilon)/B_n,
 \qquad
 \mathcal W_n(h)=\sup\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_*)}{L\|x_0-x_*\|^2}.
\]

The alternative is a certified asymptotic separation for the full function class. We retain W = 2R, the original chosen minimizer, the original radius, the actual final iterate, all finite dimensions, and every predetermined nonnegative word. The exact benchmark includes **every** integer split k+l=n-1:

\[
 C_0=B_0=1,\quad C_n=\max_{k+l=n-1}\Omega(C_k,C_l),\quad
 B_n=\max_{k+l=n-1}G(C_k,B_l),\quad U_n^{\rm ZJ}=B_n^{-1},
\]
\[
 \Omega(x,y)=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,\qquad
 G(x,y)=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2.
\]

Read in full: this TASK, OPERATOR.md, PROBLEM.md, R9_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md, R10_PARENT_VARIABLE_WALL_NOTE_20261009.md, and inverse_pp_joint_attainability_20261009_r10/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md. Checked source hashes:

- TASK: `428b28ce15e41780f95b0796f7be6e21a5c1a6b2f611a4994bb2042d983f2164`.
- Parent variable-wall note: `8a77974afa9995b0758ec77c96305de0d732dc1722680339c4823c9a8d725040`.
- Previous inverse-policy proof: `3b719f94f9f2c8afcdab61869fda6fefcebf6b4f1eee1af51788c09802a84f6b`.

The previous invocation's two policies are not renewed. The accepted-project comparison B_(n_m)/D_m < 47/50 for m >= 1536 is used only with the scope reported in the supplied handoff; it was not independently reverified here. All new results below remain UNVERIFIED.

For the diagnostic hierarchy, fix one enclosing m >= 1536 and use its parameters in every child:

\[
 \sigma=1+\sqrt2,\quad \varepsilon=1/(4m),\quad
 \beta_h=1-\varepsilon,\quad c=\sigma(1+\varepsilon),\quad
 a_h=\beta_h/c,
\]
\[
 H_0=\varnothing,\qquad H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j),\quad
 d_j=a_hc^j,\quad S_j=\sum H_j,\quad D_j=1+2S_j.
\]

The scalar a_h is unrelated to the coordinate wall vector a below. Put

\[
 u=\frac{a_h}{c-2}.
\]

The exact sum and product formulas are

\[
 S_j+1=uc^j+(1-u)2^j,\qquad
 P_j:=\prod_{h\in H_j}(h-1)=\beta_h^{2^j-1}c^{-j},
 \qquad d_jP_j=\beta_h^{2^j}/c<1/c.
 \tag{1.1}
\]

Here 1/2 < u < 1, 2 < c < 17/7 < 5/2, and D_j < 2c^j for j>=1. For example u<1 follows from c(c-2)>1>beta_h; u>1/2 follows already from beta_h>3/4, c<5/2, and c-2<1/2. These elementary bounds, rather than a numerical recurrence, will suffice.

## 2. Approach 1: build and spend a finite pool of movable coordinates

### 2.1 One fixed function and the records that must be preserved

On a finite weighted probability space, work in the Euclidean space E={X: E X=0}, with squared norm E X^2. For fixed a_i>=0 and E a>0, define

\[
 K_a=\{g\in E:g_i\ge-a_i\},\qquad
 F_a(X)=\max_{g\in K_a}\{\mathbb E[Xg]-\tfrac12\mathbb E g^2\}.
\]

Its gradient is the Euclidean projection onto K_a. It is convex and globally 1-smooth, and 0 is a valid minimizer with value 0. The weighted isometry X_i -> sqrt(p_i)X_i gives an ordinary finite-dimensional Euclidean instance. All radii below are relative to this original 0.

**Radius scope.** When every wall is positive this minimizer is unique. When some walls are zero, the minimum set may contain other points. Every exclusion in Section 3 concerns the selected original minimizer **0** and the denominator ||X_0-0||^2. It does not bound a supremum over other selected minima, nor a denominator measured to the closest point of a polar minimum cone. A different final-body minimizer has not been constructed or analyzed here. Increasing a zero wall can shrink that cone, so an earlier choice of another minimizer cannot simply be retained through a repair.

At a query X, its projection is g_i=max(X_i-t,-a_i), E g=0. An atom is called free in the current backward record when it is unsaturated at **every** recorded later physical query. If a proof also uses auxiliary queries, they must be included in that record. Increasing its wall then preserves those recorded projections and values exactly, as in the parent note.

The following construction uses direct original-variance accounting, and does not invoke any auxiliary primitive-potential equality. Its required auxiliary record is therefore empty. If optional auxiliary queries are retained, only atoms unsaturated at those queries can remain in the movable pool. The all-depth obstruction below applies even before imposing those extra restrictions.

### 2.2 An actual backward step, with a reserve that can be consumed

Suppose a finite mean-zero next law Y and its entire future trajectory have been built. Partition its atoms into a nonempty movable pool F and a frozen set D. Maintain:

1. every atom in F is free in the full later record;
2. every atom in D has Y_i<=0;
3. walls of atoms in D will never be altered.

At a reverse step h=1+d>1, choose any subset J of F to retire, leaving F'=F\J nonempty. Set D'=D union J. For each newly retired atom choose

\[
 a_i' =\max\{a_i,(Y_i)_+/d\},\qquad i\in J.
 \tag{2.1}
\]

These increases preserve the whole later record because J was free. Keep all frozen walls unchanged. With A_D=E[a' 1_(D')] and p_F=P(F'), put

\[
 t=\frac{dA_D-\mathbb E[Y1_{D'}]}{p_F}.
 \tag{2.2}
\]

Every summand d a_i'-Y_i on D' is nonnegative, so t>=0. Now increase the remaining free walls, if necessary, to

\[
 a_i'\leftarrow\max\{a_i',(Y_i-t)/d\},\qquad i\in F'.
 \tag{2.3}
\]

This again preserves every old recorded query. It does not change (2.2), which depends only on D'. Define the predecessor by

\[
 X_i=\begin{cases}
 Y_i-ha_i',&i\in D',\\
 ht/d-Y_i/d,&i\in F'.
 \end{cases}
 \tag{2.4}
\]

This is the parent's genuine inverse with omega=1_(F'). Indeed, with Z=t+da'-Y,

\[
 Z\ge0,\qquad
 \mathbb E[1_{F'}Z]=p_Ft+d\mathbb E[a'1_{F'}]-\mathbb E[Y1_{F'}]
 =d\mathbb E a'.
\]

Thus E X=0; its actual projection at threshold t is -a' on D' and X-t on F'; and X-h P_(K_a')X=Y atom by atom. Atoms in F' are also unsaturated at this newly added query. Atoms in D' satisfy X_i<=0: for old frozen atoms this is immediate, and for newly retired positive Y_i, (2.1) gives X_i<=-Y_i/d<0. Therefore the three invariants hold for the next reverse step.

There is no omitted cap exception: (2.1)--(2.4) hold for every d>0, both d<1 and d>=1. A reflected free atom can become positive or negative; it remains free and is repaired by (2.3) if needed. A frozen atom is never reflected again. No saturation event is erased.

After a finite word, all wall labels, weights and X_0 have been fixed. Forward execution uses this one final function. Later wall modifications have preserved every previously constructed physical iterate and function value; there is no changing objective during that forward run.

### 2.3 A concrete multivalue terminal reserve and exact radius accounting

For a word of length n with every step >1, take M>=n+1 free atoms. Give one frozen atom mass 1/2 and terminal value -q. Give the M free atoms masses 1/(2M) and terminal values q+eta_i, where

\[
 q>2,\quad \sum_i\eta_i=0,\quad |\eta_i|\le1/2.
\]

Initially use wall 1 everywhere. The terminal threshold is q-1. The frozen gradient is -1, and the free gradients are 1+eta_i. Thus all M reserve atoms are genuinely unsaturated at the terminal query. Retiring one atom per reverse step leaves at least one movable atom throughout. This gives a complete deterministic construction, with a genuinely multivalue terminal law if the eta_i are not all equal.

Writing overline(eta^2)=M^-1 sum eta_i^2, its preserved terminal value and variance are

\[
 2F_a(X_n)=2q-1+\tfrac12\overline{\eta^2},\qquad
 \|X_n\|^2=q^2+\tfrac12\overline{\eta^2}.
 \tag{2.5}
\]

At every reverse step, the exact original-metric cost of the new predecessor is

\[
 \|X\|^2=
 \mathbb E[1_{D'}(Y-ha')^2]
 +d^{-2}\mathbb E[1_{F'}(ht-Y)^2].
 \tag{2.6}
\]

In particular the term h^2 E[1_(D') (a')^2] is paid. Large walls on rare newly retired atoms are not free merely because their terminal gradients stay unchanged. Equations (2.1)--(2.6), applied to the complete given word, specify actual finite values and an exact radius; they are not an unproved radius bound.

If desired, choose q so that (2.5) equals B_(n_m). The construction then has terminal benchmark value exactly B_(n_m). The next result shows why its radius cannot meet the required benchmark estimate on H_m.

## 3. All-depth obstruction for the entire monotone-face class

The recursion above has active sets that only expand in forward time. This is a restriction on the actual physical trajectory, not a guess about its endpoint law. The following derivation covers every K_a trajectory with that property, including arbitrary finite weights, arbitrary nonnegative walls, arbitrary terminal laws and arbitrary numbers of atoms.

### 3.1 Orthogonal geometry of nested simplex faces

For a nonempty coordinate set A, let

\[
 p_A=P(A),\quad b_A=\frac{\mathbb E[a1_{A^c}]}{p_A},\qquad
 (e_A)_i=\begin{cases}b_A,&i\in A,\\-a_i,&i\notin A,\end{cases}
\]
\[
 L_A=\{v:\operatorname{supp}v\subset A,\ \mathbb E v=0\}.
\]

The minimum-norm point on the face with g=-a off A is e_A. Its affine hull is e_A+L_A, and e_A is orthogonal to L_A. If the projection of X lies on this face and is unsaturated on A, then

\[
 g=e_A+P_{L_A}X.
 \tag{3.1}
\]

For A contained in B, e_A-e_B lies in L_B and is orthogonal to L_A and e_B. Refining a nested chain one added atom at a time therefore gives mutually orthogonal nonzero increments

\[
 v=e_A-e_{A\cup\{i\}}.
\]

Each such v is a supporting normal of K_a on the smaller face: its largest coordinate is constant on A, and all other coordinates are smaller. Since g+a>=0 and E(g+a)=E a,

\[
 \langle g,v\rangle\le\|v\|^2\quad(g\in K_a),
 \tag{3.2}
\]

with equality on that smaller face. This uses the actual weighted Euclidean geometry; no non-isometric coordinate transform is inserted.

### 3.2 Simultaneous releases and their chronology

Suppose the actual active sets satisfy

\[
 A_0\subseteq A_1\subseteq\cdots\subseteq A_n.
 \tag{3.3}
\]

Boundary atoms may be assigned consistently to either side. At a step from A=A_(r-1) to B=A_r, define the old slack for each entering atom by

\[
 s_i=t_{r-1}-a_i-X_{r-1,i}\ge0.
\]

Refine this simultaneous release by adding the entering atoms in nondecreasing order of s_i. This refinement introduces no extra physical time or query; it only chooses an orthogonal basis.

For an intermediate set A'=A union J and the next entering atom i, direct averaging at the old query gives

\[
 \operatorname{mean}_{A'} X_{r-1}-X_{r-1,i}
 =b_{A'}+a_i+s_i-\frac{\mathbb E[1_Js]}{p_{A'}}.
 \tag{3.4}
\]

The last two terms have nonnegative difference by the chosen order. For the associated nonzero increment v, its normalized X coefficient is

\[
 \xi_{r-1}:=\frac{\langle X_{r-1},v\rangle}{\|v\|^2}
 =\frac{\operatorname{mean}_{A'}X_{r-1}-X_{r-1,i}}{b_{A'}+a_i}
 \ge1.
 \tag{3.5}
\]

At all earlier physical queries the actual face is contained in the smaller refined face, so the gradient coefficient on v is exactly 1. At and after time r it equals the X coefficient, by (3.1). At time r, (3.2) gives xi_r<=1. If S_<r denotes the sum of steps preceding the release step and that step is h=1+d, these facts give the exact chronology

\[
 \xi_{r-1}=\xi_0-S_{<r}\ge1,\qquad
 \xi_r=\xi_0-S_{<r}-h\le1,
\]
\[
 S_{<r}+1\le\xi_0\le S_{<r}+h+1.
 \tag{3.6}
\]

After the release, xi is multiplied by 1-h_t at every subsequent step. Thus the contribution of this entire mode to terminal twice-value, divided by its original squared radius contribution, is at most

\[
 \max\left\{
 \frac{d^2\prod_{\text{after }r}(h_t-1)^2}{(S_{<r}+1)^2},\quad
 \frac{\prod_{\text{after }r}(h_t-1)^2}{(S_{<r}+h+1)^2}
 \right\}.
 \tag{3.7}
\]

The maximum occurs at an endpoint of (3.6), because |1-(S_<r+h)/xi_0| has its maximum there. Importantly, an atom that enters late cannot bring an arbitrary uncharged quadratic deviation: (3.4)--(3.6) tie that deviation to its original radius.

For completeness, zero walls do not create a missing uncharged direction. A zero increment means b_(A')=a_i=0. All walls outside A' are then zero. Use the contrast direction positive and constant on A', negative on i, and zero elsewhere. Its old gradient coefficient is zero. By (3.4) and the same slack ordering its old X coefficient is nonnegative. Feasibility of every g in K_a makes its gradient coefficient nonpositive. After i is active the gradient coefficient equals the X coefficient; that X coefficient did not change in the release step because its old gradient coefficient was zero. It must therefore vanish. Its original coefficient also vanishes. Such directions can be discarded, rather than divided by a zero norm.

### 3.3 The remaining modes and exact original-radius comparison

Directions in L_(A_0) are quadratic from time 0 and have terminal-to-initial squared ratio product_t(h_t-1)^2. The last face has an unreleased normal e=e_(A_n). If e is nonzero and xi_t=<X_t,e>/||e||^2, its gradient coefficient is 1 throughout, so xi_n=xi_0-S_n. Projection optimality against 0 gives xi_n>=1. Its terminal twice-value is

\[
 (2\xi_n-1)\|e\|^2,
\]

and its original radius contribution is xi_0^2||e||^2. Their ratio satisfies

\[
 \frac{2(\xi_0-S_n)-1}{\xi_0^2}\le\frac1{1+2S_n}.
 \tag{3.8}
\]

Any residual original component orthogonal to L_(A_n) and e is orthogonal to every gradient in the chain; it stays constant and contributes only nonnegative additional radius. At the terminal point the envelope formula is exactly

\[
 2F_a(X_n)=\|P_{L_{A_n}}X_n\|^2
            +2\langle X_n,e\rangle-\|e\|^2.
 \tag{3.9}
\]

Equations (3.7)--(3.9) therefore bound the actual full ratio by the largest mode ratio. There is no replacement of the original radius by a radius at a release time.

### 3.4 Evaluate the bound on the complete H_m

Every suffix product of the positive excesses h-1 in H_j is at most 1. This follows by induction: a suffix either lies in the right child, or contains that child and the bridge, whose product is d_(j-1)P_(j-1)<1/c, times a suffix of the left child. Empty suffixes have product 1.

Let E_j be the largest of the two **unsquared** expressions in (3.7), over release steps anywhere in the complete H_j, with the real prefix sums in that word. For a release in its left child, the two quantities are multiplied by d_(j-1)P_(j-1)<1/c. At the bridge and throughout the right child, the denominators are at least 1+S_(j-1), while the required suffix products are at most 1. Therefore

\[
 E_j\le\max\{E_{j-1}/c,\ (1+S_{j-1})^{-1}\},\qquad E_0=0,
\]
\[
 E_j\le\frac1{u c^{j-1}}\quad(j\ge1).
 \tag{3.10}
\]

Consequently every released-mode payoff on H_m is at most

\[
 E_m^2\le(c^2/u^2)c^{-2m},
\]

and every initially quadratic mode is at most P_m^2<c^(-2m). For m>=1536 these are below 1/D_m: it suffices that c^m>2c^2/u^2, whose right side is <50, whereas c^m>2^m. The affine bound (3.8) is 1/D_m. Thus

\[
 \boxed{\quad
 \frac{2F_a(X_n)}{\|X_0\|^2}\le\frac1{D_m}
 \quad\text{for every nested-active-set }K_a\text{ trajectory on }H_m.
 \quad}
 \tag{3.11}
\]

The standard constant-gradient Huber trajectory attains 1/D_m, so this upper bound within the stated trajectory class **with selected minimizer 0** is exact. In particular the concrete reserve recursion in Section 2 obeys

\[
 \|X_0\|^2\ge D_m\,[2F_a(X_n)].
\]

At terminal benchmark value B=B_(n_m), it requires original variance at least B D_m; its benchmark-normalized payoff is at most B/D_m<47/50. This rules out the whole monotone-reserve invariant, even with arbitrarily many terminal values and different walls. Adding old auxiliary-query preservation requirements only restricts this class further.

The conclusion does **not** cover a trajectory that leaves and later reenters an active face. It does not prove W_(n_m)(H_m)<=1/D_m for general functions or for all K_a trajectories.

## 4. Approach 2: pay for a real change between opposite saturated faces

The preceding obstruction forces actual recycling, so this approach abandons nested active sets. It uses a different fixed body when multiple reserves are combined: an orthogonal product of interval projection bodies. Each component executes the whole physical word and has a fully charged original radius.

### 4.1 An exact paid-reflection component for an arbitrary word

Take any predetermined nonnegative word and a selected step h_i=1+d>1. Write

\[
 P=\sum_{t<i}h_t,\qquad R=\sum_{t>i}h_t.
\]

For A>0 define the asymmetric Huber function

\[
 f_A(x)=\max_{g\in[-A,1]}\{xg-g^2/2\}.
\]

It is convex, 1-smooth, and minimized at the original 0. Its gradient is clip(x,[-A,1]). It is also a two-atom K_a instance in the weighted mean-zero metric: take X=(x,-x), equal weights, and a=(A,1).

Choose any q>=1, and fix

\[
 A=(q+R)/d,\qquad x_0=-A(1+P).
 \tag{4.1}
\]

Throughout the prefix the gradient is -A and the trajectory ends at x_i=-A. The chosen step gives

\[
 x_{i+1}=dA=q+R.
\]

Throughout the suffix the gradient is 1, and the actual terminal value is x_n=q. The two opposite gradient faces are attained; clipping is strict at the initial query when P>0, and at the post-bridge query when q+R>1. In particular both strict events occur at an internal bridge of H_m with nonempty children. Every step, including zeros elsewhere in the word, is accounted for. The wall A is chosen once before forward execution.

The exact original radius and terminal twice-value are

\[
 x_0^2=A^2(1+P)^2,\qquad 2f_A(x_n)=2q-1,
\]

so the paid payoff is

\[
 \frac{d^2(2q-1)}{(1+P)^2(q+R)^2}.
\]

Its maximum over q>=1 is attained at q=R+1. Thus a real full-word witness has

\[
 \boxed{\quad
 L_i(h)=\frac{(h_i-1)^2}{(1+P)^2(1+2R)},\quad
 A=\frac{1+2R}{h_i-1},\quad x_0=-A(1+P),\quad x_n=1+R.
 \quad}
 \tag{4.2}
\]

The factor A^2(1+P)^2 is exactly the cost of preparing the reflected reserve through the entire prefix. It cannot be replaced by the radius at x_i=-A. This is an actual face-change construction, not an increase of a formerly saturated wall while pretending its old query survives.

### 4.2 A recursive bank with several terminal values

One can build a finite reserve bank on any binary parse of the physical word: at each node whose bridge exceeds 1, add a component (4.2), using the bridge's **global** P and R in the complete word; recurse into the left and right node lists. Nodes with bridge at most 1 add no reflection component. Also retain a baseline Huber component with payoff 1/(1+2 sum h).

For arbitrary positive spatial scales lambda_i, set

\[
 F(x)=\sum_i\lambda_i^2 f_{A_i}(x_i/\lambda_i),
 \qquad (x_0)_i=\lambda_i[-A_i(1+P_i)].
 \tag{4.3}
\]

The gradient is 1-Lipschitz in the ordinary product Euclidean norm. The selected minimizer is 0. Every component runs the same entire physical word without a reset. Its terminal coordinate is lambda_i(1+R_i), so the bank can have many terminal values and many actual face changes at different times. Its original squared radius and terminal objective are exactly the sums of the component quantities.

Consequently its total normalized payoff is the radius-weighted average of its component payoffs. The bank cannot exceed their largest value. This statement is about the explicit product body (4.3); it is not a claim that a coupled simplex K_a decomposes into independent reservoirs.

### 4.3 Quantitative obstruction on every node of the complete H_m

Consider a step at level j in H_m. Its excess is d=a_h c^j. Put s=S_j. The actual global prefix and suffix sums satisfy P>=s and R>=s, because the occurrence has a full H_j child on each side. Also

\[
 \frac d{1+s}\le c-2=:r<3/7.
 \tag{4.4}
\]

Let T=P+R=S_m-(1+d). At fixed T, the denominator in (4.2) is

\[
 (1+P)^2[1+2(T-P)].
\]

Its logarithm is concave on s<=P<=T-s, so its minimum occurs at an endpoint. The smaller endpoint is P=s: the function (1+x)^2/(1+2x) is increasing for x>=0. Therefore

\[
 D_m L_i(H_m)
 \le\frac{d^2}{(1+s)^2}\,
 \frac{D_m}{D_m-2s-2(1+d)}.
\]

The last fraction decreases as D_m increases, and the actual condition R>=s gives D_m>=4s+2d+3. Hence

\[
 D_m L_i(H_m)
 \le\left(\frac d{1+s}\right)^2
       \frac{4s+2d+3}{2s+1}
 \le r^2(3+2r)
 <\frac{243}{343}<1.
 \tag{4.5}
\]

This covers every occurrence and every inherited level with the same enclosing parameters. Even after q and its wall A are optimized, every paid-reflection component in this bank is uniformly worse than the standard Huber component. Any finite positive combination has payoff at most 1/D_m, and hence benchmark-normalized payoff below 47/50.

At the root alone, (4.2) gives the particularly transparent complete-H_m witness

\[
 \frac{d_{m-1}^2}{(1+S_{m-1})^2D_{m-1}}.
\]

It has the correct order c^(-m), but a fixed loss. Formula (4.5), rather than this order statement, is the decisive reason this recursive independent-bank repair cannot close the target.

## 5. What is closed and what is still missing

Approach 1 supplies a genuine reserve recursion that can be executed at every reverse step on H_m, with all eventual wall labels fixed before forward execution and with exact original-variance accounting. Its tempting invariant is quantitatively insufficient: the entire nested-active-set class at the selected original minimum 0 has exact best payoff 1/D_m. Persistent physical unsaturation is therefore not by itself the missing target-level mechanism. Old auxiliary queries cannot repair this loss.

Approach 2 changes the physical mechanism: it crosses opposite saturated faces and pays the preparation radius. Its full-word witnesses and multivalue product bank are realizable, and (4.5) excludes the entire specified bank on H_m. The failure is the cost of the globally prepared reflected coordinates, rather than an unsupported claim that their wall labels were free.

Neither derivation excludes a **coupled** multivalue K_a trajectory with repeated exits and reentries of active faces. Such a trajectory would have to exploit correlations between reserve coordinates that are absent from (4.3), and it would not obey the orthogonal one-release decomposition used in (3.11). No construction or benchmark-scale original-radius upper bound for that coupled mechanism has been obtained in this invocation.

In particular there is no proved uniform all-word lower bound (1-o(1))/B_n, no full-class asymptotic upper certificate, and no complete candidate for formal audit. The unchanged low-mass Huber argument still covers sum h<=(B_n-1)/2. The exact all-integer-split DP has not been replaced by the diagnostic hierarchy.

## 6. Resource and closeout record

- Assigned native model invocation: 1, gpt-6-astra / ultra.
- Substantive approaches: 2, completed to the scoped outcomes above.
- Mathematical programs, numerical arrays, solvers: 0.
- Formal submissions and verifier calls: 0.
- Additional agents/models, renewals, worker or service starts: 0.
- Access: designated public sources, operating instructions, writing skill, and a bounded memory-registry check; no private worker memory.
- Writes: this PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md in the assigned packet only.
- Budget, Goal, truth stores, OPERATOR, CURRENT_RUN, all other packets, publication, finalization and revocation: untouched.
- Exact token usage is not exposed to this child. The parent owns the authoritative backend/budget receipt; no token count is invented.

Stop after this bounded allocation. Original target ACTIVE / OPEN. All new mathematics UNVERIFIED.

They want direct all-depth/all-word closure inspired by Zhang--Jiang, not more equivalent descriptions or fixed-horizon toys. This fresh call uses a NEW actual operation, not another renewal of greedy policies. The original Goal is ACTIVE/OPEN. Consult OFF.

Original target is lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1 or a certified full-function-class asymptotic separation, W=2R, original selected minimizer/radius and actual last iterate, every finite dimension and every nonnegative predetermined word. Keep exact C_n/B_n recurrences and ALL integer splits from PROBLEM. A lower witness needs a uniform all-word bound (1-o(1))/B_n. The baseline already covers all words with total mass <=(B_n-1)/2 by a standard Huber trajectory. Do not solve the wrong direction with a restricted-family upper bound.

The NEW parent construction is K_a={g:Eg=0,g_i>=-a_i} on a finite weighted mean-zero Euclidean space, with fixed nonnegative a, a_bar=Ea>0. Projection g=max(X-t,-a), Eg0. Inverting h=1+d>1 uses Z=t+d*a-Y>=0 and E[omega Z]=d*a_bar, X_L=Y-h*a, X_R=t-a+Z/d. Splits duplicate the ENTIRE already built future. This defines one static finite-dimensional function; original radius is Var X0 at the valid original minimum0.

For local cost C_(alpha,beta)(X)=VarX+2alpha F_a(X)+beta||P_Ka X||², C0=1+alpha+beta>d²,T=h+alpha,w=C0/d²-1,gamma=d+2T/w, choose R=Y+gamma*a, E(R-z)_+=gamma*a_bar. Select all R>z, optional boundary fraction, p=P(I),a_I=E[a1_I], and t=z+(d+gamma)(a_bar-a_I)/p. Under t>=max(Y-d*a), the ACTUAL inverse attains

 C_(alpha,beta)(X)=VarY+T U_gamma(Y)+gamma(wd+T)||P_Ka(Y/gamma)||².

This gives the exact PP root when alpha=C_L-1,beta=C_L(C_L-1),d(C_L+C_R+d)=C_L*C_R. It is still a guarded one-step identity, not the desired theorem.

Crucial genuine operation: a selected atom violating Y_i-d*a_i<=t necessarily has Y_i>0. If that atom was UNSATURATED at every already-built future physical and required auxiliary query, its wall a_i may be increased until the guard holds. This changes neither those future projections/values nor current p,z,t or q=P(Y/gamma). The same inverse cost is attained. The complete proof and cross-moment accounting are in the parent note. A previously strictly saturated atom cannot be modified for free. Physical-query preservation alone does not preserve arbitrary past auxiliary bookkeeping.

Your primary task is to turn this operation into a whole-word construction: choose a finite terminal shape with a reserve of genuinely unsaturated coordinates, and prove that each required guard repair occurs on such coordinates (or pay explicitly for using an already saturated coordinate). Track the exact original variance and actual terminal objective. Do not merely name a persistent-unsaturation invariant: show how every inverse step/join preserves it, including reflected atoms, cap steps, and old auxiliary queries. The final wall labels must be chosen before forward execution. No changing function, erasing a saturation event, resetting a child, ignoring a cross-time row, or free change of auxiliary scale.

Two existing policies are quantitatively excluded on the COMPLETE inherited family, so change the actual construction. Fix m>=1536, eps=1/(4m), beta_h=1-eps,c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps),a_h=beta_h/c, H0empty,H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_h*c^j,H_j), SAME enclosing parameters. Set n_m=2^m-1,D_m=1+2sumH_m,Q=(B_(n_m)+1)/2. A plausible concrete direction is a terminal law containing both affine payoff atoms and several unsaturated reservoir atoms, with different wall labels. Aim first for an actual whole-H_m benchmark-scale witness as a decisive viability test, then for the all-word rule using the exact DP capacities. A full-H_m witness alone is not the original theorem. # Bounded full primitive discovery and three exact original witnesses

Status: COMPLETE JOINT RESEARCH DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max3 round41,
the first of the two rounds allocated after completed40, with completed
ceiling42 and attempted ceiling43. No fact_submit is authorized or called.
The original exact-profile problem, with W=2R, remains OPEN.

Accepted inputs are fact04219554e41d9750, Section10, for the full primitive
identity; fact1252b8823bb5a290 for the original interpolation orientation;
and fact4c92d3fc4eb6153e for the conditional fixed-b3 capacity thresholds.
Every new mathematical assertion used in this document is proved jointly.
The earlier C80 and max2 longer-seed drafts suggested witnesses but are
not premises. Numerical infeasibility is not an exact proof.

## 1. Finite experiment, scope and resources

The PUBLIC directory containing this document retains the stdlib atomic
meter, eight pre-registered exact word plans, every Wolfram request,
complete raw result, parsed copy and final-accounting receipt. The original
model uses all r(r+1) ordered off-diagonal Lambda entries, with no rank,
support, reflection, scalar-factor or zero-remainder restriction. Every
original gradient remains formal and distinct. This is primitive
certificate feasibility, not full-objective PEP.

Both charged calibrations passed: h=(1) has Lambda01=2,Lambda10=4 and
the nonzero PSD matrix [[1,-1],[-1,1]]; h=(2) is exactly rejected by
the diagonal contradiction in Section2. The backend was existing Wolfram
Language15.0.1/CSDP, tolerance10^-8. No package was installed.

The frozen discovery words and actual outcomes were:

| Name | Physical word h | Capacity A | Registered screens | Conic outcome |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| f5a | (3/2,12/5,6/5,12/5,7/5) | 99/10 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f5b | (31/20,47/20,5/4,12/5,27/20) | 99/10 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f5c | (3/2,5/2,23/20,12/5,27/20) | 99/10 | global cut fails | no solve |
| f5d | (31/20,5/2,11/10,12/5,27/20) | 99/10 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f6a | (3/2,5/2,5/4,39/20,5/2,131/100) | 1201/100 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f6b | (159/100,5/2,13/10,191/100,5/2,121/100) | 1201/100 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f6c | (3/2,49/20,5/4,103/50,5/2,5/4) | 1201/100 | adjacent pair fails | no solve |
| f6d | (31/20,49/20,5/4,201/100,5/2,5/4) | 1201/100 | all pass | numerical infeasible |

All six conic rejections have the raw warnings pinf/nsolc, Infinity
objective and Indeterminate multipliers. No numerical array or eigenvalue
was silently converted into a certificate or impossibility theorem.
Sections4--6 independently give exact actual-function rejections of f5c,
f6c and f5a. The other five rows remain numerical rejection reports only.

There were10 charged cases:2 calibrations and8 distinct discovery words
in2 families. Eight cases used one conic solve each; two used none.
Measured kernel construction/solve/readback time was1.972763 seconds.
A conservative0.5-second export/local-postprocessing guard per case,
which is not a claimed actual duration, makes6.972763 accounted seconds.
Discovery stopped at case10. With no credible reconstruction target,
the remaining2 slots and83.027237 seconds were retired and the ledger
was closed. No old ledger changed, and no correction retry occurred.

All exact witness work below is textual proof construction, with no
additional evaluator, numerical check, machine algebra or coefficient
reconstruction job. No additional physical proposal is assessed here.

## 2. Exact equivalence of the implemented original PSD model

For a word h=(h_0,...,h_(r-1))>=0 set s=sum h_i and A=1+s. Let e be
the all-ones column of length r+1, T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, with
the other entries zero. Set

 L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<r,-s(s+2)),
 M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2.

The original constraints are Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for every i!=j,
L^T e=omega, and M>=0 on all of R^(r+1).

Under the original flow, e^T D e=-sum h_i^2-s^2, while

 e^T LT e=omega^T T e
   =-2sum_i h_i(1+sum_(t<i)h_t)+2s(1+s)
   =sum_i h_i^2+s^2.

Thus e^T M e=0. If M is PSD then M e=0: for every real vector v,
nonnegativity of (e+t v)^T M(e+t v) for both signs of sufficiently
small real t forces v^T M e=0. Conversely, when M e=0, use the
invertible matrix P=[e_0,...,e_(r-1),e], where e_i are coordinate
columns. The exact congruence is P^T M P=diag(M[0:r,0:r],0).
Consequently the implemented equations M e=0 and the leading r-square
PSD constraint are equivalent to the FULL original PSD constraint.
They impose no extra rank or support restriction. The original complete
M, every sign and every flow row are retained in the raw readback.

For h=(1), the array [[0,2],[4,0]] gives flow(-2,2) and
M=[[1,-1],[-1,1]], with quadratic(u-v)^2. For h=(2), writing
Lambda01=a>=0, flow gives Lambda10=a+4 and M=[[-a,2],[2,a-4]].
PSD would force a<=0 and a>=4. This contradiction is independent of
the solver and completes the two calibration references.

## 3. Full original identity and the actual-function witness method

For physical GD x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i g_i with g_i=grad F(x_i), write
f_i=F(x_i) and use the accepted orientation

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

The primitive energy is

 V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)
       +sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_r||^2.

The cited accepted identity states, under the original flow,

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>.

Therefore any ACTUAL legal trajectory with V_h<0 excludes every
original nonnegative Lambda with full PSD M: the left side would be
negative and the right side nonnegative. No support selection is needed.

Here is a common complete construction for the planar witnesses.
Let K be a compact convex set containing0 in its interior and define

 F(x)=max_(g in K){<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.

Completing the square shows that the unique maximizing g is the
Euclidean projection P_K(x). The two projection normal inequalities at
x and x' imply ||P_K(x)-P_K(x')||^2<=<P_K(x)-P_K(x'),x-x'>, hence the
projection is1-Lipschitz. The maximum formulas bound the differentiability
remainder between0 and ||P_K(x+z)-P_K(x)|| ||z||<=||z||^2. Thus F is
convex, differentiable and1-smooth, with grad F=P_K. It has unique
minimum F(0)=0: for x!=0 a sufficiently small positive epsilon has
epsilon x in K and gives F(x)>=epsilon(1-epsilon/2)||x||^2>0.

If the displayed g_i are projections, let y_i=x_i-g_i. Then
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2 and direct expansion gives

 Q_ij=<g_i-g_j,y_j><=0

for EVERY ordered pair, by the projection normal inequality at j.
The optimum jet is x_star=g_star=f_star=0. Its two directions satisfy
Q_(i,star)=0 and Q_(star,i)=-<g_i,y_i><=0 because0 belongs to K.
This specifies all remote, terminal and optimum rows, not just adjacent
ones. Telescoping ||x_i||^2-||x_(i+1)||^2 gives

 V_h=||x_0||^2-||x_r+s g_r||^2.

In the constructions below x_r=g_r, so V_h=||x_0||^2-A^2||g_r||^2.
The unique minimizer is0, hence the true squared initial distance is
exactly ||x_0||^2 in every case.

The scalar screens used the interval version K=[-eta,1]: the derivative
is clip(x,[-eta,1]). At a selected step b>1, let P=1+prefix sum and
D=1+suffix sum, and choose eta=(b-1)/D. Start x_0=P. Gradients are1
before and at b, then -eta through the terminal point x_r=-eta.
Every point is on the claimed constant-gradient side of this SAME
function. Its energy is P^2-A^2(b-1)^2/D^2. Thus the original global
cut is A(b-1)<=PD. Steps<=1 contribute a vacuous nonnegative test.

The other scalar screens use the exact convex quadratic F_z(x)=z x^2/2,
z in{1/4,1/2,3/4,1}, x_0=1. With p_0=1 and
p_(i+1)=(1-z h_i)p_i, substitution gives

 V_h(z)=sum_i z h_i(1+z-z h_i)p_i^2
                 -s z[1+A z]p_r^2.

At z=1 this reduces to1-A^2 product_i(1-h_i)^2. Testing those four
curvatures makes no assertion about every curvature or nonlinear law.

For completeness, the adjacent-pair screen is also an original actual
function. Let the pair be b,c>1, put B=b-1,C=c-1,
P=1+prefix sum,D=1+suffix sum,U=b+c+D-1. If D>BC, set

 kappa=B^2 C+U(D-BC), t=B^2 C/kappa,
 u=(1,0), w=(t,sqrt(t(1-t))), v=-(B u+D w)/c,
 K=conv{u,v,w}.

Then0<t<1 and positive weights B,c,D express0 as an interior
convex combination. Prefix queries lie at positive multiples>=1 of u,
the two pair updates are u -> -B u -> D w, and suffix queries lie at
positive multiples>=1 of w. Their gradients are respectively u,v,w.
At -B u, with y=-B u-v, the exact comparisons are

 <w-v,y>=[-B^2 C+kappa t]/c^2=0,
 <u-v,y>=-B(1-t)C/c<0.

At a prefix normal a u, a>=0, both comparisons to v-u and w-u are
nonpositive. At a suffix normal a w, a>=0, the comparisons to u-w
and v-w are0 and -a t[1+(B+D)/c]<=0. Thus Section3's full row formula
applies and V_h=P^2-A^2 t, where A=P+U. Expansion gives

 A^2 B^2 C-P^2 kappa
       =U{BC[P^2+2BP+BU]-P^2 D}.

The resulting necessary inequality is P^2 D>=BC[P^2+2BP+BU].
The evaluator also contains a stop at D<=BC, recalled from the earlier
C80 draft. This document does not import that unverified branch: EVERY
applicable adjacent pair of EVERY registered word had D>BC, as the
retained exact raw outputs show. No D<=BC rejection occurred. Only the
fully proved D>BC branch was used in this finite batch or in this proof.

## 4. Exact scalar rejection of f5c

Take h=(3/2,5/2,23/20,12/5,27/20), A=99/10. At physical index1,
P=5/2,D=59/10 and eta=15/59. The scalar function is explicitly

 F(x)=x-1/2                    for x>=1,
 F(x)=x^2/2                    for -eta<=x<=1,
 F(x)=-eta*x-eta^2/2            for x<=-eta.

It is convex differentiable1-smooth, with unique minimizer0. The entire
trajectory is

 x=(5/2,1,-3/2,-285/236,-141/236,-15/59),
 g=(1,1,-15/59,-15/59,-15/59,-15/59).

Each listed update uses its original physical step, and every point
lies in the stated branch. The values are F(x_i); Section3 supplies
all original and optimum interpolation residuals. The true squared
distance is25/4. The global cut slack is

 PD-A(b-1)=(5/2)(59/10)-(99/10)(3/2)=-1/10.

The exact original primitive energy is

 V_h=(5/2)^2-(99/10)^2(15/59)^2
       =(295^2-297^2)/118^2=-296/3481<0.

Thus f5c cannot have any full original primitive certificate.

## 5. Exact planar rejection of f6c

Take h=(3/2,49/20,5/4,103/50,5/2,5/4), A=1201/100, and the
adjacent pair at physical indices3,4. The parameters of Section3 are

 P=31/5, B=53/50, C=3/2, D=9/4, U=581/100,
 D-BC=33/50, kappa=138/25, t=2809/9200.

Use u,w,v,K,F as in Section3; here v=-(53/125)u-(9/10)w.
The COMPLETE original trajectory and gradients are

 x=((31/5)u,(47/10)u,(9/4)u,u,-(53/50)u,(9/4)w,w),
 g=(u,u,u,u,v,w,w).

Every update is exact. Section3 checks each projection normal, every
ordered interpolation row and both optimum directions. Its unique
minimum is0, with true squared initial distance961/25. The adjacent
pair slack is -2660367/500000. More directly, the actual energy is

 V_h=961/25-(1201/100)^2(2809/9200)
       =-515224409/92000000<0.

This excludes every original42-entry/fullR7 certificate for f6c.

## 6. Exact rejection of the screen-passing word f5a

This is a textual counterexample construction motivated by a numerical
rejection, not a reconstruction of floating dual coefficients. Take

 h=(3/2,12/5,6/5,12/5,7/5), s=89/10, A=99/10,
 u=(1,0), v=(-2/5,3/10), w=(3/37,-839/4218),
 p=w+u+v, q=w+v, K=conv{p,q,w}.

The exact scalar products are

 ||u||^2=1, ||v||^2=1/4, <u,v>=-2/5,
 <w,u>=3/37, <w,v>=-7/76.

Put alpha=1165/6327 and beta=4195/6327. Coordinate substitution gives
w=-alpha u-beta v. The three barycentric coefficients alpha,
beta-alpha=3030/6327 and1-beta=2132/6327 are positive, so0 is interior
to K. Section3 makes F(x)=max_(g in K)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2) a single
globally convex differentiable1-smooth function with unique minimum0.

Let P=39/10, H=15/2 and start at

 x_0=A w+P u+H v=(63/37,987/3515).

Use the original five steps and propose g=(p,p,q,q,w,w). Directly
subtracting P p+(18/5)q+(7/5)w from x_0 ends at x_5=w. The normals
y_i=x_i-g_i are specified by

 y_1=(37/5)w+(7/5)u+5v,       y_0=y_1+(3/2)p,
 y_3=(19/5)w+(7/5)v,         y_2=y_3+(6/5)q,
 y_4=(7/5)w,                y_5=0.

Their complete two-direction table is

| i | g_i | <y_i,u> | <y_i,v> |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | p | 189/185 | -337/950 |
| 1 | p | 0 | 4/475 |
| 2 | q | -587/925 | 18/95 |
| 3 | q | -233/925 | 0 |
| 4 | w | 21/185 | -49/380 |
| 5 | w | 0 | 0 |

At p the necessary vertex comparisons are <y,u>>=0 and
<y,u+v>>=0. Row0's sum is23441/35150>0; row1's sum is4/475>0.
At q they are <y,u><=0 and <y,v>>=0, both satisfied in rows2,3.
At w they are <y,v><=0 and <y,u+v><=0; row4's sum is
-217/14060<0, and row5 is zero. Thus every proposed gradient is
indeed the projection at the corresponding physical iterate. All
original Q_ij and optimum rows follow from Section3, with no suppressed
sample or identification of formal gradient variables in the certificate.

The true squared initial distance is exactly
(63/37)^2+(987/3515)^2>0. The actual primitive energy telescopes to

 V_h=||A w+P u+H v||^2-A^2||w||^2
    =P^2-(4/5)PH+H^2/4+2A[(3/37)P-(7/76)H]
    =2349/400-1042767/140600
    =-434187/281200<0.

Consequently f5a is exactly excluded from the entire original30-entry,
fullR6 certificate cone, even with arbitrary nonzero PSD remainder.
This strengthens this ONE row beyond its numerical status.

For comparison, its complete original global-cut slacks were
(69/20,57/50,1077/50,39/50,227/50)>0. Its unit-curvature slack was
15530299/39062500>0. Every adjacent-pair test and all four registered
quadratic energies also passed, with exact data retained in f5a_raw.json.
Passing those necessary tests therefore does not ensure full feasibility.

## 7. What remains

No actual full seed at the accepted capacities has been constructed.
Only the eight listed proposals were tested, and only f5a,f5c,f6c have
the complete exact rejections supplied here. The numerical ledger is
CLOSED. The unrestricted original Goal is still OPEN, and nothing here

# Bounded full primitive discovery and four exact original witnesses

Status: COMPLETE JOINT RESEARCH DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max3 round41,
the first of the two rounds allocated after completed40, with completed
ceiling42 and attempted ceiling43. No fact_submit is authorized or called.
The original exact-profile problem, with W=2R, remains OPEN.

Accepted inputs are fact04219554e41d9750, Section10, for the full primitive
identity; fact1252b8823bb5a290 for the original interpolation orientation;
and fact4c92d3fc4eb6153e for the conditional fixed-b3 capacity thresholds.
Every new mathematical assertion used in this document is proved jointly.
The earlier C80 and max2 longer-seed drafts suggested witnesses but are
not premises. Numerical infeasibility is not an exact proof.

## 1. Finite experiment, scope and resources

The PUBLIC directory containing this document retains the stdlib atomic
meter, eight pre-registered exact word plans, every Wolfram request,
complete raw result, parsed copy and final-accounting receipt. The original
model uses all r(r+1) ordered off-diagonal Lambda entries, with no rank,
support, reflection, scalar-factor or zero-remainder restriction. Every
original gradient remains formal and distinct. This is primitive
certificate feasibility, not full-objective PEP.

Both charged calibrations passed: h=(1) has Lambda01=2,Lambda10=4 and
the nonzero PSD matrix [[1,-1],[-1,1]]; h=(2) is exactly rejected by
the diagonal contradiction in Section2. The backend was existing Wolfram
Language15.0.1/CSDP, tolerance10^-8. No package was installed.

The frozen discovery words and actual outcomes were:

| Name | Physical word h | Capacity A | Registered screens | Conic outcome |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| f5a | (3/2,12/5,6/5,12/5,7/5) | 99/10 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f5b | (31/20,47/20,5/4,12/5,27/20) | 99/10 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f5c | (3/2,5/2,23/20,12/5,27/20) | 99/10 | global cut fails | no solve |
| f5d | (31/20,5/2,11/10,12/5,27/20) | 99/10 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f6a | (3/2,5/2,5/4,39/20,5/2,131/100) | 1201/100 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f6b | (159/100,5/2,13/10,191/100,5/2,121/100) | 1201/100 | all pass | numerical infeasible |
| f6c | (3/2,49/20,5/4,103/50,5/2,5/4) | 1201/100 | adjacent pair fails | no solve |
| f6d | (31/20,49/20,5/4,201/100,5/2,5/4) | 1201/100 | all pass | numerical infeasible |

All six conic rejections have the raw warnings pinf/nsolc, Infinity
objective and Indeterminate multipliers. No numerical array or eigenvalue
was silently converted into a certificate or impossibility theorem.
Sections4--7 independently give exact actual-function rejections of f5c,
f6c,f5a and f6a. The other four rows remain numerical rejection reports only.

There were10 charged cases:2 calibrations and8 distinct discovery words
in2 families. Eight cases used one conic solve each; two used none.
Measured kernel construction/solve/readback time was1.972763 seconds.
A conservative0.5-second export/local-postprocessing guard per case,
which is not a claimed actual duration, makes6.972763 accounted seconds.
Discovery stopped at case10. With no credible reconstruction target,
the remaining2 slots and83.027237 seconds were retired and the ledger
was closed. No old ledger changed, and no correction retry occurred.

All exact witness work below is textual proof construction, with no
additional evaluator, numerical check, machine algebra or coefficient
reconstruction job. No additional physical proposal is assessed here.

## 2. Exact equivalence of the implemented original PSD model

For a word h=(h_0,...,h_(r-1))>=0 set s=sum h_i and A=1+s. Let e be
the all-ones column of length r+1, T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, with
the other entries zero. Set

 L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,
 D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<r,-s(s+2)),
 M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2.

The original constraints are Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for every i!=j,
L^T e=omega, and M>=0 on all of R^(r+1).

Under the original flow, e^T D e=-sum h_i^2-s^2, while

 e^T LT e=omega^T T e
   =-2sum_i h_i(1+sum_(t<i)h_t)+2s(1+s)
   =sum_i h_i^2+s^2.

Thus e^T M e=0. If M is PSD then M e=0: for every real vector v,
nonnegativity of (e+t v)^T M(e+t v) for both signs of sufficiently
small real t forces v^T M e=0. Conversely, when M e=0, use the
invertible matrix P=[e_0,...,e_(r-1),e], where e_i are coordinate
columns. The exact congruence is P^T M P=diag(M[0:r,0:r],0).
Consequently the implemented equations M e=0 and the leading r-square
PSD constraint are equivalent to the FULL original PSD constraint.
They impose no extra rank or support restriction. The original complete
M, every sign and every flow row are retained in the raw readback.

For h=(1), the array [[0,2],[4,0]] gives flow(-2,2) and
M=[[1,-1],[-1,1]], with quadratic(u-v)^2. For h=(2), writing
Lambda01=a>=0, flow gives Lambda10=a+4 and M=[[-a,2],[2,a-4]].
PSD would force a<=0 and a>=4. This contradiction is independent of
the solver and completes the two calibration references.

## 3. Full original identity and the actual-function witness method

For physical GD x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i g_i with g_i=grad F(x_i), write
f_i=F(x_i) and use the accepted orientation

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

The primitive energy is

 V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)
       +sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_r||^2.

The cited accepted identity states, under the original flow,

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>.

Therefore any ACTUAL legal trajectory with V_h<0 excludes every
original nonnegative Lambda with full PSD M: the left side would be
negative and the right side nonnegative. No support selection is needed.

Here is a common complete construction for the planar witnesses.
Let K be a compact convex set containing0 in its interior and define

 F(x)=max_(g in K){<x,g>-||g||^2/2}.

Completing the square shows that the unique maximizing g is the
Euclidean projection P_K(x). The two projection normal inequalities at
x and x' imply ||P_K(x)-P_K(x')||^2<=<P_K(x)-P_K(x'),x-x'>, hence the
projection is1-Lipschitz. The maximum formulas bound the differentiability
remainder between0 and ||P_K(x+z)-P_K(x)|| ||z||<=||z||^2. Thus F is
convex, differentiable and1-smooth, with grad F=P_K. It has unique
minimum F(0)=0: for x!=0 a sufficiently small positive epsilon has
epsilon x in K and gives F(x)>=epsilon(1-epsilon/2)||x||^2>0.

If the displayed g_i are projections, let y_i=x_i-g_i. Then
f_i=<x_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2 and direct expansion gives

 Q_ij=<g_i-g_j,y_j><=0

for EVERY ordered pair, by the projection normal inequality at j.
The optimum jet is x_star=g_star=f_star=0. Its two directions satisfy
Q_(i,star)=0 and Q_(star,i)=-<g_i,y_i><=0 because0 belongs to K.
This specifies all remote, terminal and optimum rows, not just adjacent
ones. Telescoping ||x_i||^2-||x_(i+1)||^2 gives

 V_h=||x_0||^2-||x_r+s g_r||^2.

In the constructions below x_r=g_r, so V_h=||x_0||^2-A^2||g_r||^2.
The unique minimizer is0, hence the true squared initial distance is
exactly ||x_0||^2 in every case.

The scalar screens used the interval version K=[-eta,1]: the derivative
is clip(x,[-eta,1]). At a selected step b>1, let P=1+prefix sum and
D=1+suffix sum, and choose eta=(b-1)/D. Start x_0=P. Gradients are1
before and at b, then -eta through the terminal point x_r=-eta.
Every point is on the claimed constant-gradient side of this SAME
function. Its energy is P^2-A^2(b-1)^2/D^2. Thus the original global
cut is A(b-1)<=PD. Steps<=1 contribute a vacuous nonnegative test.

The other scalar screens use the exact convex quadratic F_z(x)=z x^2/2,
z in{1/4,1/2,3/4,1}, x_0=1. With p_0=1 and
p_(i+1)=(1-z h_i)p_i, substitution gives

 V_h(z)=sum_i z h_i(1+z-z h_i)p_i^2
                 -s z[1+A z]p_r^2.

At z=1 this reduces to1-A^2 product_i(1-h_i)^2. Testing those four
curvatures makes no assertion about every curvature or nonlinear law.

For completeness, the adjacent-pair screen is also an original actual
function. Let the pair be b,c>1, put B=b-1,C=c-1,
P=1+prefix sum,D=1+suffix sum,U=b+c+D-1. If D>BC, set

 kappa=B^2 C+U(D-BC), t=B^2 C/kappa,
 u=(1,0), w=(t,sqrt(t(1-t))), v=-(B u+D w)/c,
 K=conv{u,v,w}.

Then0<t<1 and positive weights B,c,D express0 as an interior
convex combination. Prefix queries lie at positive multiples>=1 of u,
the two pair updates are u -> -B u -> D w, and suffix queries lie at
positive multiples>=1 of w. Their gradients are respectively u,v,w.
At -B u, with y=-B u-v, the exact comparisons are

 <w-v,y>=[-B^2 C+kappa t]/c^2=0,
 <u-v,y>=-B(1-t)C/c<0.

At a prefix normal a u, a>=0, both comparisons to v-u and w-u are
nonpositive. At a suffix normal a w, a>=0, the comparisons to u-w
and v-w are0 and -a t[1+(B+D)/c]<=0. Thus Section3's full row formula
applies and V_h=P^2-A^2 t, where A=P+U. Expansion gives

 A^2 B^2 C-P^2 kappa
       =U{BC[P^2+2BP+BU]-P^2 D}.

The resulting necessary inequality is P^2 D>=BC[P^2+2BP+BU].
The evaluator also contains a stop at D<=BC, recalled from the earlier
C80 draft. This document does not import that unverified branch: EVERY
applicable adjacent pair of EVERY registered word had D>BC, as the
retained exact raw outputs show. No D<=BC rejection occurred. Only the
fully proved D>BC branch was used in this finite batch or in this proof.

## 4. Exact scalar rejection of f5c

Take h=(3/2,5/2,23/20,12/5,27/20), A=99/10. At physical index1,
P=5/2,D=59/10 and eta=15/59. The scalar function is explicitly

 F(x)=x-1/2                    for x>=1,
 F(x)=x^2/2                    for -eta<=x<=1,
 F(x)=-eta*x-eta^2/2            for x<=-eta.

It is convex differentiable1-smooth, with unique minimizer0. The entire
trajectory is

 x=(5/2,1,-3/2,-285/236,-141/236,-15/59),
 g=(1,1,-15/59,-15/59,-15/59,-15/59).

Each listed update uses its original physical step, and every point
lies in the stated branch. The values are F(x_i); Section3 supplies
all original and optimum interpolation residuals. The true squared
distance is25/4. The global cut slack is

 PD-A(b-1)=(5/2)(59/10)-(99/10)(3/2)=-1/10.

The exact original primitive energy is

 V_h=(5/2)^2-(99/10)^2(15/59)^2
       =(295^2-297^2)/118^2=-296/3481<0.

Thus f5c cannot have any full original primitive certificate.

## 5. Exact planar rejection of f6c

 x=((31/5)u,(47/10)u,(9/4)u,u,-(53/50)u,(9/4)w,w),
 g=(u,u,u,u,v,w,w).

Every update is exact. Section3 checks each projection normal, every

minimum is0, with true squared initial distance961/25. The adjacent

 V_h=961/25-(1201/100)^2(2809/9200)
       =-515224409/92000000<0.

This excludes every original42-entry/fullR7 certificate for f6c.

## 6. Exact rejection of the screen-passing word f5a

This is a textual counterexample construction motivated by a numerical
rejection, not a reconstruction of floating dual coefficients. Take

 h=(3/2,12/5,6/5,12/5,7/5), s=89/10, A=99/10,
 u=(1,0), v=(-2/5,3/10), w=(3/37,-839/4218),
 p=w+u+v, q=w+v, K=conv{p,q,w}.

The exact scalar products are

 ||u||^2=1, ||v||^2=1/4, <u,v>=-2/5,
 <w,u>=3/37, <w,v>=-7/76.

Put alpha=1165/6327 and beta=4195/6327. Coordinate substitution gives
w=-alpha u-beta v. The three barycentric coefficients alpha,
beta-alpha=3030/6327 and1-beta=2132/6327 are positive, so0 is interior
to K. Section3 makes F(x)=max_(g in K)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2) a single
globally convex differentiable1-smooth function with unique minimum0.

Let P=39/10, H=15/2 and start at

 x_0=A w+P u+H v=(63/37,987/3515).

Use the original five steps and propose g=(p,p,q,q,w,w). Directly
subtracting P p+(18/5)q+(7/5)w from x_0 ends at x_5=w. The normals
y_i=x_i-g_i are specified by

 y_1=(37/5)w+(7/5)u+5v,       y_0=y_1+(3/2)p,
 y_3=(19/5)w+(7/5)v,         y_2=y_3+(6/5)q,
 y_4=(7/5)w,                y_5=0.

Their complete two-direction table is

| i | g_i | <y_i,u> | <y_i,v> |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | p | 189/185 | -337/950 |
| 1 | p | 0 | 4/475 |
| 2 | q | -587/925 | 18/95 |
| 3 | q | -233/925 | 0 |
| 4 | w | 21/185 | -49/380 |
| 5 | w | 0 | 0 |

At p the necessary vertex comparisons are <y,u>>=0 and
<y,u+v>>=0. Row0's sum is23441/35150>0; row1's sum is4/475>0.
At q they are <y,u><=0 and <y,v>>=0, both satisfied in rows2,3.
At w they are <y,v><=0 and <y,u+v><=0; row4's sum is
-217/14060<0, and row5 is zero. Thus every proposed gradient is
indeed the projection at the corresponding physical iterate. All
original Q_ij and optimum rows follow from Section3, with no suppressed
sample or identification of formal gradient variables in the certificate.

The true squared initial distance is exactly
(63/37)^2+(987/3515)^2>0. The actual primitive energy telescopes to

 V_h=||A w+P u+H v||^2-A^2||w||^2
    =P^2-(4/5)PH+H^2/4+2A[(3/37)P-(7/76)H]
    =2349/400-1042767/140600
    =-434187/281200<0.

Consequently f5a is exactly excluded from the entire original30-entry,
fullR6 certificate cone, even with arbitrary nonzero PSD remainder.
This strengthens this ONE row beyond its numerical status.

For comparison, its complete original global-cut slacks were
(69/20,57/50,1077/50,39/50,227/50)>0. Its unit-curvature slack was
15530299/39062500>0. Every adjacent-pair test and all four registered

Passing those necessary tests therefore does not ensure full feasibility.

## 7. Exact rejection of the screen-passing six-step word f6a

Take

 h=(3/2,5/2,5/4,39/20,5/2,131/100),
 s=1101/100, A=1201/100,
 u=(1,0), v=(-3/10,2/5), w=(22/317,-31243/161036),

Here

 ||u||^2=1, ||v||^2=1/4, <u,v>=-3/10,
 <w,u>=22/317, <w,v>=-25/254.

Set alpha=49025/644144 and beta=312430/644144. Direct coordinates
give w=-alpha u-beta v, and the three barycentric weights are
alpha, beta-alpha=263405/644144, and1-beta=331714/644144, all positive.

Put P=4,H=97/10 and start at x_0=A w+P u+H v. Its first coordinate
is A*(22/317)+109/100>0, so the true squared initial distance
||x_0||^2 is positive. The proposed full gradient sequence is

 (g_0,...,g_6)=(p,p,q,q,q,w,w).

The total weights on p,q,w in the six physical updates are respectively

 y_1=(951/100)w+(3/2)u+(36/5)v, y_0=y_1+(3/2)p,
 y_4=(381/100)w+(3/2)v,
 y_3=y_4+(39/20)q, y_2=y_4+(16/5)q,
 y_5=(131/100)w, y_6=0.

These formulas specify all original queries by x_i=g_i+y_i and use
every original step. To check the projection cone at p, the exact
first-switch normal satisfies

 <y_1,u>=0, <y_1,v>=2103/5080>0.

Also <p,u>=2439/3170>0 and
<p,u+v>=13/20+22/317-25/254>11/20>0, since25/254<1/10.
Thus both p rows satisfy <y_i,u>>=0 and <y_i,u+v>>=0.

At q the final-switch normal satisfies

 <y_4,u>=-5883/31700<0, <y_4,v>=0.

Moreover <q,u>=-731/3170<0 and <q,v>=77/508>0. Adding the
nonnegative multiples of q in y_2,y_3 preserves both q cone tests:

At w, <w,v>=-25/254<0 and

 <w,u+v>=22/317-25/254=-2337/80518<0.

Thus y_5 and y_6 satisfy both w cone tests. Every gradient is now

                         +2A[(22/317)P-(25/254)H]

    =-353759/16103600<0.

This excludes every original42-entry/fullR7 certificate for f6a.
It is an additional actual-function obstruction beyond all registered

## 8. What remains

No actual full seed at the accepted capacities has been constructed.
Only the eight listed proposals were tested, and only f5a,f5c,f6a,f6c have

CLOSED. The unrestricted original Goal is still OPEN, and nothing here
is a new accepted fact. No formal gate or additional computation is
authorized by this document.

One already allocated analytical round remains after this handoff. A

in Sections6--7 extend to the other screen-passing five/six-step words,

# Original-coordinate model and calibration references

Research derivation, not a verifier-accepted fact. Accepted interface:
fact4c92d3fc4eb6153e. No formal gate is authorized in this allocation.

The evaluator retains every off-diagonal Lambda entry as a free variable,
with its original nonnegative sign; diagonal entries are zero by definition.
The original full flow is retained without dropping its final row.
All matrices T, L, D and M are exactly those in the assignment.

Under the original flow, e^T M e=0: the D term is
-sum(h_i^2)-s^2. The remaining scalar is omega^T T e, which equals
-2 sum_i h_i(1+sum_(t<i)h_t)+2s(1+s)=sum(h_i^2)+s^2.
For a symmetric positive semidefinite M, e^T M e=0 implies M e=0:
positivity of (e+t v)^T M(e+t v) for both signs of arbitrarily small t
forces v^T M e=0 for every v. Conversely, if M e=0, the invertible
matrix P=[e_0,...,e_(r-1),e] gives P^T M P=diag(M[0:r,0:r],0).
Thus the exact original PSD constraint is equivalent to M e=0 together
with PSD of its leading r-square principal block. This does not remove
any original formal gradient or identify any pair. Full M is read back.

For h=(1), choose Lambda=[[0,2],[4,0]]. Then
T=[[1,0],[1,1]], L=[[2,-2],[-4,4]], omega=(-2,2), and
M=[[1,-1],[-1,1]]. Its quadratic is (u-v)^2, nonzero and PSD.
For h=(2), write Lambda_01=a>=0. Original flow gives Lambda_10=a+4.
The full M is [[-a,2],[2,a-4]]. PSD would require both diagonal entries
nonnegative, hence a<=0 and a>=4, a contradiction. These are separate
textual exact references for the two charged computational calibrations.

Wolfram's documented VectorGreaterEqual[{matrix,0},{"SemidefiniteCone",r}]
syntax is used in one SemidefiniteOptimization call, with linear original
signs, flow and null-vector equations. No objective PEP is involved.
Documentation: https://reference.wolfram.com/language/ref/SemidefiniteOptimization.html
The required Wolfram context retrieval automatically included unrelated
WolframAlpha symbolic snippets despite an explicit request for no evaluation.
Those snippets were not used as a calibration, screen or mathematical result;
every actual Language evaluator invocation is separately reserved and charged.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001499 -->
# Cursor for the second already allocated round

Read TASK.md and the latest master_guidance first. Completed-after-return
is expected41, with ceiling42; the outer loop owns the actual counters.
The numerical ledger is CLOSED at10 cases, all8 distinct proposals used,
with the remaining2 slots retired. No computational or formal credit is
available from that ledger. No new proposal list or numerical restart.

Final joint draft14532189ad44824c reproduces four exact original-cone
counterexamples. It is UNVERIFIED, not a premise. Reuse it only as
awareness or reproduce its needed derivations in a new joint argument.
Accepted inputs remain04219554e41d9750,1252b8823bb5a290,4c92d3fc4eb6153e.

The other four registered words f5b,f5d,f6b,f6d have only numerical
infeasibility reports. An immediate useful textual subgoal is to decide
their full original projection-normal inequalities without any evaluator,
or identify exactly which normal inequality fails for the current law.
This is not a request to prove a whole-family no-seed theorem from a
finite batch, and not an authorization to enlarge the computational list.

Candidate general three-vertex parametrization (working derivation):
take h=(a,b,m_1,...,m_t,c,d), with t=1 or2 and b,c>1. Put

 R=sum m_i, s=a+b+R+c+d, A=1+s,
 P=a+b, H=P+R+c=s-d,
 J=b+R+c+d=s-a, C=b+R+c-1, E=c+d.

Choose k>0 and q>k^2, u=(1,0), v=(-k,sqrt(q-k^2)). Set

 x=(C k-(b-1))/J, y=-(c-1)q/E,
 w=(x,(y+kx)/sqrt(q-k^2)),
 p=w+u+v, q_vertex=w+v,
 beta=-(y+kx)/(q-k^2), alpha=k beta-x.

If0<alpha<beta<1 then0 is interior to conv{p,q_vertex,w}.
Start x0=A w+P u+H v; the gradient pattern is p,p, then q_vertex
at every middle-block query and at c, then w,w. It ends at w.

The two switch normals are

 Y_first=J w+(b-1)u+C v,
 Y_last=E w+(c-1)v.

Their u/v contacts are respectively zero by construction. Sufficient
remaining projection tests are

 J y-(b-1)k+Cq>=0,
 E x-(c-1)k<=0,
 x-k<=0, y+q>=0, y<=0, x+y<=0,
 x+1-k>=0, x+y+1-2k+q>=0.

The intermediate normals add nonnegative multiples of q_vertex; the
initial normal adds a p, and the tail normal is d w. Thus these signs
cover every original physical query. The actual primitive energy is

 V=P^2-2kPH+qH^2+2A(Px+Hy).

Sections6--7 of the final joint draft contain complete instances:
f5a uses k=2/5,q=1/4 and has V=-434187/281200;
f6a uses k=3/10,q=1/4 and has V=-353759/16103600.
No formula in this cursor is a verified standalone lemma. Any future
use needs its complete derivation retained jointly or a separately
authorized verifier gate. Do not mistake a failed choice of k,q for
failure of the whole full-PSD cone.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001500 -->
# Frozen derivation for the full weighted-prefix PEP

Prepared analytically before freeze or any mathematical program. Allocation `6b000743076cec7f`; model `gpt-6-astra`, effort `ultra`. Numerical results will be UNVERIFIED DISCOVERY. Original target OPEN.

## 1. Normalized full finite model

For a prescribed prefix `k=(k_0,...,k_(p-1))`, introduce all formal vectors `(x0,g0,...,g_p)` and their full symmetric Gram matrix `G`. Its size is `p+2`; no rank restriction is imposed. Use `G>=0`, `G_00<=1`, function values `f_0,...,f_p`, and the zero minimizer jet. Physical points are `x_i=x0-sum_(t<i)k_t g_t`. Include ALL distinct ordered pairs among `star,0,...,p`:

`Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 <=0`.

The objective is exactly `V=2 f_p-(1-a_last^2)||g_p||^2`. The final prefix gradient is in this criterion, and every incoming row at that query is retained. There is no prescribed source weight, dual support, tree, common column, diagonal remainder, or residual-rank restriction.

For production, fix the single enclosing `m_env=1536`, `eps=1/(4m_env)`, `beta=1-eps`, `c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+eps)`, `a=beta/c`. Build only each charged case's requested word from `H_0=(), H_(r+1)=(H_r,1+a*c^r,H_r)`. The prefixes are `P_j=H_j` with only its last step removed, for `j=3,4,5`. Use `a_last=a`, `D_j=1+2 sum H_j`, and report `D_j V` against `17/16`.

## 2. Why the exact finite model represents the function class

For a convex differentiable 1-smooth function, the standard smooth-convex inequality, derived in accepted `1252b8823bb5a290`, is exactly `Q_ij<=0`. This includes both minimizer directions. Every actual normalized trajectory is therefore feasible.

Conversely, an exactly PSD finite Gram matrix has a realization by vectors in dimension at most `p+2`. For exactly feasible values and all ordered rows, put `c_i=<g_i,x_i>-f_i` and

`H(v)=max_i [c_i+<x_i,v-g_i>+||v-g_i||^2/2]`, including the zero minimizer jet.

Then `H(v)-||v||^2/2` is convex. Its conjugate `F(x)=sup_v(<x,v>-H(v))` is finite everywhere, convex, differentiable, and has 1-Lipschitz gradient: strong convexity gives a unique maximizer `g(x)`, and the two strong subgradient inequalities give `<x-x',g(x)-g(x')> >= ||g(x)-g(x')||^2`. The conjugate difference bounds then give differentiability with this gradient.

At a supplied jet `i`, the difference between the `j`th quadratic and `c_i` at `g_i` is exactly `Q_ij<=0`; its own quadratic is active and has derivative `x_i`. Thus `F(x_i)=f_i` and `grad F(x_i)=g_i`. The zero jet gives `F(0)=min F=0`. The supplied GD relations are consequently the actual updates for this same function. This construction uses all rows, including the final prefix query; no terminal deletion is invoked.

The exact feasible set is bounded by the fixed-word bounds `||x_i||,||g_i|| <= product_(t<i)(1+k_t)` and `0<=f_i<=||x_i||^2/2`, so its closed Gram/value formulation attains the maximum. Scaling vectors by a positive radius and values by its square establishes the original-radius normalization; a zero-radius feasible trajectory has zero criterion. This exact correspondence does not make a numerically indefinite or interpolation-defective Gram matrix realizable. Raw numerical outputs are not exact witnesses.

## 3. Calibration 1: empty prefix, a_last=1/2, value 4/7

The minimizer row gives `f_0<=<x0,g0>-||g0||^2/2`. Therefore

`2 f_0-(3/4)||g0||^2 <=2<x0,g0>-(7/4)||g0||^2 <=(4/7)||x0||^2`.

The exact certificate, with `lambda_(0,star)=2`, is

`(4/7)||x0||^2-2 f_0+(3/4)||g0||^2+2Q_(0,star) = (7/4)||g0-(4/7)x0||^2`.

For `t=4/7`, define the global one-dimensional Huber function

`F_t(x)=x^2/2` for `|x|<=t`, and `F_t(x)=t|x|-t^2/2` otherwise.

Its derivative is `clip(x,-t,t)`, continuous, nondecreasing and 1-Lipschitz. Hence the function is convex and 1-smooth, with minimum zero attained at zero. At `x0=1`, `g0=4/7`, `f0=20/49`, `Q_(0,star)=0`, `Q_(star,0)=-12/49`, and the objective is `40/49-12/49=4/7`. Radius is one and the certificate square vanishes. This establishes the exact optimum before numerical work.

## 4. Calibration 2: prefix (1), a_last=0, value 1/4

Set `x1=x0-g0`. The original rows expand to

`Q_(0,star)=f0-<x0,g0>+||g0||^2/2`,

`Q_(1,star)=f1-<x0,g1>+<g0,g1>+||g1||^2/2`,

`Q_01=f0-f1-||g0||^2/2-<g0,g1>+||g1||^2/2`,

`Q_10=f1-f0+||g0||^2/2+||g1||^2/2`.

With `lambda_(0,star)=1/2`, `lambda_(1,star)=3/2`, `lambda_01=1/2`, `lambda_10=1`, and all other multipliers zero, the value coefficients cancel. The remaining quadratic coefficients are

`||x0||^2/4-<x0,g0>/2-3<x0,g1>/2+||g0||^2/2+<g0,g1>+5||g1||^2/2`.

These are exactly the expansion of

`||x0-g0-3g1||^2/4+||g0-g1||^2/4`.

Thus the proposed original-row certificate holds as a formal identity and proves the upper bound `1/4`.

For the same global Huber definition, now take `t=1/4`. Starting at one and using the step one gives `x1=3/4`, `g0=g1=1/4`, `f0=7/32`, `f1=5/32`. The two outgoing minimizer rows and both chronological rows are zero; `Q_(star,0)=-3/16`, `Q_(star,1)=-1/8`. The objective is `2(5/32)-1/16=1/4`, and both certificate squares vanish. Every original interpolation/minimum condition and the original radius are therefore checked directly.

## 5. Exact fixture and numerical gates

Within each charged calibration process, independently form rational original-row coefficients with `Fraction`. Check every coefficient of each displayed certificate against its square expansion, all function-value flow coefficients, both exact Huber transcripts against the listed signed row values, the objective attainment, and radius. Every exact algebraic difference must be zero. The displayed rational coefficients, including `4/7`, are never certified by floating tolerances.

Also retain independent raw floating coefficient/attainment differences; require their maximum absolute difference at most `1e-7`, not exact floating zero. Both calibrations require `optimal`, objective error at most `1e-7`, original row/radius defect at most `1e-7`, raw minimum Gram eigenvalue at least `-1e-7`, all exact fixture differences zero, and complete exports. Failure stops production and retires unused slots.

## 6. Dual diagnostics without a second solve

If each original row is `A_r:G+B_r f`, its primal constraint multiplier is `lambda_r`. Let `q_dual` be the radius multiplier and `Z` the matrix multiplier for `G>=0`. Export, without repair,

`flow=B^T lambda-2 e_p`,

`M=q_dual E_00+(1-a_last^2)E_(p+1,p+1)+sum_r lambda_r A_r`,

`matrix_stationarity=M-Z`, all eigenvalues of `G,M,Z`, all signed rows, `G_00-1`, `-lambda`, and all complementary products. Preserve `q_dual-V` and native canonical primal/dual gaps with their raw signs. A positive computed dual margin is not exact feasibility.

For the actual last step `b=1+a_last`, smoothness gives `2 f_after <= V` by `2b-b^2=1-a_last^2`. A numerical failure of `V<=17/(16D)` does not imply failure of the actual after-step objective; the inequality is only an upper bound on that objective. No new last-step function value is invented.

## 7. Frozen resource protocol

Exactly two calibration slots and three production slots, one solve per slot. Each entire child group is precharged 20 seconds and externally deadline-protected, including imports, builds, exact checks, canonicalization, the sole CLARABEL solve, diagnostics, optional extraction and all exports. Existing runtime only; no tests/probes, numerical work, imports of mathematical packages, or array reads outside those groups. No retry, fallback, repair, extra case or postclosure array analysis. Preparation and broker use only administrative standard-library/file/hash/accounting operations.

Freeze this derivation, task versions, cases, schema, child, broker, preparation source, registration/budget snapshots, primary mathematical inputs, and separate host/container context snapshots before any precharge. No postfreeze source changes. The broker closes after the fixed batch or an earlier calibration failure. Original target OPEN.

# Handoff: full weighted-prefix finite PEP

**UNVERIFIED DISCOVERY. Goal ACTIVE, original target OPEN.** Allocation `6b000743076cec7f` is CLOSED. This is a fresh grant, separate from the completed analytical allocation `bfc190fee04e9d8d`.

## What ran

One native `gpt-6-astra` / `ultra` invocation, one batch, exactly five precharged whole-process slots of 20 seconds each. One CVXPY/CLARABEL solve per slot; no retry/fallback. Both calibrations passed, then `P_3,P_4,P_5` ran using the same enclosing `m_env=1536` parameters. All statuses were `optimal` / `Solved`, all export sets complete, all warning lists empty.

The full primal Gram PEP maximized

`V_pref=2 f_p-(1-a_last^2)||g_p||^2`

under original radius `||x0||^2<=1`, the full PSD Gram on `(x0,g0,...,g_p)`, and **every** ordered smooth-convex interpolation row on `star,0,...,p`. No rank, source-weight, common-column, tree, diagonal-residual, or support condition was imposed. Every incoming row at the final prefix query was kept.

For production, `eps=1/(4*1536)`, `beta=1-eps`, `c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+eps)`, `a=beta/c`, `H_0=()`, `H_(r+1)=(H_r,1+a*c^r,H_r)`. Each prefix deletes only the actual final step `1+a`; `D_j=1+2 sum H_j`. Full words, prefixes and matrices are saved.

## Exact calibration premises

The empty-prefix calibration has `a_last=1/2` and exact optimum `4/7`, with certificate

`(4/7)||x0||^2-2f0+(3/4)||g0||^2+2Q_(0,star)=(7/4)||g0-(4/7)x0||^2`.

A global Huber function with cap `4/7`, at `x0=1`, attains it. The second calibration has prefix `(1)`, `a_last=0`, exact optimum `1/4`, and multipliers `(0,star):1/2`, `(1,star):3/2`, `(0,1):1/2`, `(1,0):1`. Its remainder is

`||x0-g0-3g1||^2/4+||g0-g1||^2/4`.

A cap-`1/4` Huber function attains it with `g0=g1=1/4`, `x1=3/4`. `DERIVATION.md` proves global convexity, 1-smoothness, attained minimum, all signed rows and exact attainment before freeze. Charged `Fraction` checks returned zero exact differences; raw floating maximum was `2.7755575615628914e-17`, preserved under the `1e-7` gate.

Saved calibration objective errors: `9.500078501645248e-11`, `1.129529803023388e-11`. Both full gates PASS; exit zero alone was not used as the gate.

## Numerical outcome

| Case | `D_j V_pref` | `D_j q_dual` | Primal minus `17/16` |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| `P_3` | 1.079536836925768 | 1.0795368390342717 | +0.017036836925768073 |
| `P_4` | 1.0307918140421866 | 1.0307918147264237 | -0.03170818595781344 |
| `P_5` | 1.0124176155927562 | 1.0124176174000286 | -0.050082384407243774 |

Unscaled primal values: `0.03977600183919927`, `0.015398842868376537`, `0.006209991968555536`. Raw dual-minus-primal gaps: `7.768872350988332e-11`, `1.0221715834868306e-11`, `1.108549016470839e-11`.

The production raw Gram minimum eigenvalues are `-2.2697398248745334e-11`, `-3.39535029749277e-12`, `-3.639481083660732e-12`. Original signed-row maxima are positive: `3.712325585825127e-11`, `8.951519213373782e-12`, `1.465013483789579e-11`. Every original dual polynomial also has a tiny negative eigenvalue; full stationarity and all raw spectra are retained.

Accordingly there is no exact primal witness or exact dual upper certificate. Coordinate extraction was explicitly skipped for every production case; no clipping or repair occurred. The numerical `P_3` failure is of the sufficient **weighted-prefix** estimate at that finite child depth. Smoothness only says `2 f_after <= V_pref`; the failure does not supply a counterexample to the actual full-`H_3` objective. Nor does a depth-three child diagnostic disprove a statement required only at sufficiently large enclosing depth. `P_4/P_5` are finite positive evidence, not an all-depth theorem.

## Durable evidence

- `REPORT.md`: all saved comparisons, unrepaired defects, scope, normalization and resource receipt.
- `DERIVATION.md`, `CASES.json`, `OUTPUT_SCHEMA.md`, `child.py`: frozen exact model, calibration premises, fixed cases, exports and mathematical source.
- `FREEZE.json`, `PREPARATION_RECEIPT.json`, `NATIVE_REGISTRATION.json`, budget snapshots and `input_context/`: 31 execution-container-verified frozen entries and separate host/container provenance.
- `raw/<case>/source_frame.json` and `input_arrays.npz`: full literal words and all original coefficient matrices.
- `raw/<case>/arrays.npz`, `transcript.json`, `result.json`, `solver_data.json`, `canonical_data.json`, `program_data.npz`, `exact_fixtures.json`: complete raw results and diagnostics.
- Per-case precharges, receipts and logs; top-level `registration.jsonl`, `budget.json`, `CLOSED.json`: actual execution/accounting.

`9014fa480ce83890df24bc670dd6209cdf5811229fee869f4171e4f94b4847a7`.

Freeze SHA: `6bc451ca70f8cd489f9240ca7afa8e110076a95abcbc665ececb478a4423bfcd`.

## Closed resources and next boundary

Native registration exactly once; 5 jobs / 100 accounted seconds / `32.778605112998775` actual whole-child seconds. Zero jobs or seconds remain, zero unused slots, formal attempts zero. `CLOSED.json` reports `all_five_jobs_accounted`, all frozen hashes unchanged, `STOP=true`. Initial sandbox Docker access failed before registration; the authorized escalated preparation and the single fresh batch succeeded. No mathematical probe or test run preceded freeze.

# Frozen output schema

Per case:

Numerical values are not clipped or repaired. Optional coordinate extraction is deliberately skipped, with an explicit reason; negative raw eigenvalues are preserved.

- `stdout.txt`, `stderr.txt`: process and solver output; `failure.json` on an exception if writeable before the external deadline.
- `PRECHARGE.json`, `PREFLIGHT_HASH_RECEIPT.json`, `receipt.json`: frozen-byte verification, full 20-second precharge before launch, actual external time, process-group deadline/termination, exit/export/calibration status and byte hashes.
